We all found ourselves looking at Lili and she was looking so sharp herself, so ready to jump and so at bay, that it was all *I* needed, at any rate, to make Erich’s theory about the Maintainer a rock-bottom certainty.

Bruce must have realized the way our minds were working, for he started to struggle in earnest and at the same time called, “For God’s sake, don’t do anything to Lili! Let me loose, you idiots! Everything’s true I told you—I can save you from that bomb. Sevensee, you took my side against the Spiders; you’ve nothing to lose. Sid, you’re an Englishman. Beau, you’re a gentleman and you love her, too—for God’s sake, stop them!”

Beau glanced up over his shoulder at Bruce and the others surging around close to his ankles and he had on his poker face. Sid I could tell was once more going through the purgatory of decision. Beau reached his own decision first and I’ll say it for him that he acted on it fast and intelligently. Right from his kneeling position and before he’d even turned his head quite back, he jumped Erich.

But other things in this cosmos besides Man can pick sides and act fast. Illy landed on Beau midway and whipped his tentacles around him tight and they went wobbling around like a drunken white-and-silver barber pole. Beau got his hands each around a tentacle, and at the same time his face began to get purple, and I winced at what they were both going through.

Maybe Sevensee had a hoof in Sid’s purgatory, because Bruce shook loose from the satyr and tried to knock out Mark, but the Roman twisted his arm and kept him from getting in a good punch.

Erich didn’t make a move to mix into either fight, which is my little commandant all over. Using his fists on anybody but me is beneath him.

===Chinese===
卡比在我旁边开了腔：「本森-卡特是一位物质术士，原打算扮成老太婆参加行动。斗篷和兜帽都在那些衣装里。」我纳闷，这条冷血的女军官，怎么会就是十分钟前还朝马克抛黏糊糊媚眼的那个姑娘。

「怎么样？」埃里希问，瞟一眼呼叫器，随即把目光扫过我们，仿佛我们中间总该藏着几分老*Wehrmacht*（国防军）的铁骨。我们大家发觉自己都在看莉莉，而她自己也锋芒毕露，蓄势欲跳，又困兽犹斗，反正有这些就足够*我*把埃里希关于维持器的推论当成铁板钉钉了。

布鲁斯想必察觉了我们脑子的走向，因为他开始真刀真枪地挣扎，同时高喊：「看在上帝份上，别动莉莉！放开我，你们这群蠢货！我说的话句句是真的——我能把你们从那颗炸弹底下救出来。七观，你站在过我这边反蜘蛛；你没什么可输的。西德，你是英国人。博，你是绅士，你也爱她——看在上帝份上，拦住他们！」

博回头瞥了一眼布鲁斯和涌到他脚边的人群，脸上是那张扑克脸。西德呢，我看得出，又在一趟趟熬炼狱般的抉择。博先拿定了主意，而且我得替他说句公道话：他执行得又快又聪明。人就从跪姿弹起，头还没完全转回去，已经扑向了埃里希。

可这个宇宙里，除了人，别的东西也会选边站、也会动作快。伊尔利半路落在博身上，触手把他一圈圈勒紧，两人歪歪扭扭滚作一团，像一根喝醉的白银两色理发店旋柱。博两只手各攥住一条触手，与此同时他的脸开始发紫，他们俩各自受的那份罪，看得我直龇牙。

没准七观也往西德的炼狱里掺了一蹄子，因为布鲁斯从半人羊手里挣脱出来，想放倒马克，却被罗马人拧住胳膊，没让他结结实实挨上这一拳。

埃里希却没有挪一步去掺和任何一场，这才是我的小指挥官的做派。除了对我，抡拳头打人有失他的身份。

---

===Original===
Then Sid made his choice, but there was no way for me to tell what it was, for, as he reached for the Minor Maintainer, Kaby contemptuously snatched it away from his hands and gave him a knee in the belly that doubled me up in sympathy and sent him sprawling on his knees toward the fighters. On the return, Kaby gave Lili, who’d started to grab too, an effortless backhand smash that set her down on the divan.

Erich’s face lit up like an electric sign and he kept his eyes fixed on Kaby.

She crouched a little, carrying her weight on the balls of her feet and firmly cradling the Minor Maintainer in her left arm, like a basketball captain planning an offensive. Then she waved her free hand decisively to the right. I didn’t get it, but Erich did and Mark too, for Erich jumped for the Refresher sector and Mark let go of Bruce and followed him, ducking around Sevensee’s arms, who was coming back into the fight on which side I don’t know. Illy un-whipped from Beau and copied Erich and Mark with one big spring.

Then Kaby twisted a dial as far as it would go and Bruce, Beau, Sevensee and poor Siddy were slammed down and pinned to the floor by about eight gravities.

It should have been lighter near us—I hoped it was, but you couldn’t tell from watching Siddy; he went flat on his face, spreadeagled, one hand stretched toward me so close, I could have touched it (but not let go!), and his mouth was open against the floor and he was gasping through a corner of it and I could see his spine trying to sink through his belly. Bruce just managed to get his head and one shoulder up a bit, and they all made me think of a Doré illustration of the *Inferno* where the cream of the damned are frozen up to their necks in ice in the innermost circle of Hell.

The gravity didn’t catch me, although I could feel it in my left arm. I was mostly in the Refresher sector, but I dropped down flat too, partly out of a crazy compassion I have, but mostly because I didn’t want to take a chance of having Kaby knock me down.

Erich, Mark and Illy had got clear and they headed toward us. Maud picked the moment to make her play; she hadn’t much choice of times, if she wanted to make one. The Old Girl was looking it for once, but I guess the thought of her miracle must have survived alongside the fear of sacked sun and must have meant a lot to her, for she launched out fast, all set to straight-arm Kaby into the heavy gravity and grab the Minor Maintainer with the other hand.

===Chinese===
随后西德做出了他的选择，可我无从知道是哪一边，因为就在他伸手够副维持器时，卡比轻蔑地把它从他手里夺走，顺势一膝顶进他的肚子——我同病相怜地跟着弯了腰——把他顶得双膝发飘、朝缠斗的人堆栽去。回程路上，卡比又顺手给了同样伸手来抢的莉莉一记不费力的反手，把她安放在了躺椅上。

埃里希的脸亮得像霓虹灯牌，眼睛牢牢钉在卡比身上。

她微微一蹲，重心压上前脚掌，左臂稳稳搂着副维持器，活像一位筹谋进攻的篮球队长。然后她腾出的那只手朝右边干脆一挥。我没看懂，埃里希懂了，马克也懂了：埃里希直扑盥洗区，马克撒开布鲁斯跟了上去，绕过七观重新插进战团的胳膊——他这回算哪一边，我不知道。伊尔利从博身上解开触手，一个大弹跳，有样学样。

然后卡比把一个旋钮一拧到底，布鲁斯、博、七观和可怜的西迪就被大约八个重力砰然砸落，钉在了地板上。

我们近旁本该轻些——我希望是，可看西迪的样子判断不出来；他脸朝下趴平，四肢大张，一只手朝我伸过来，近得我一伸手就能碰到（可碰了也拉不住！），嘴贴着地板张开，从嘴角里喘气，我看得出他的脊椎正拼命往肚子底下沉。布鲁斯只勉强把头和一边肩膀抬起一点，他们那副样子，让我想起多雷为《地狱篇》（*Inferno*）画的插图：地狱最内环里，受诅咒者中的精华，冻在齐颈的冰里。

重力没逮住我，虽说左胳膊已经觉出了分量。我大半个身子在盥洗区，可我也跟着趴平了，一半出于我那股疯癫的恻隐，多半是不想赌卡比会不会把我撂倒。

埃里希、马克和伊尔利脱出了重压区，朝我们奔来。莫德挑了这一刻出手；她若想出手，也没多少时刻好挑。老姑娘这回可真显出了「老」字，可我猜，她那个奇迹的念头，必是在「太阳被劫」的恐惧旁边活了下来，而且分量不轻，因为她迅猛扑出，摆好架势，一只直臂要把卡比推进重压区，另一只手去夺副维持器。

## XIV “Now Will You Talk?” / 「现在你肯说了吗？」

===Original===
> Like diamonds, we are cut with our own dust.

===Chinese===
> 如同钻石，我们是被自己的粉末割开的。（译注：韦伯斯特《白魔》。）

---

===Original===
Cretans have eyes under their back hair, or let’s face it, Entertainers aren’t Soldiers. Kaby weaved to one side and flicked a helpful hand and poor old Maud went where she’d been going to send Kaby. It sickened me to see the gravity take hold and yank her down.

I could have jumped up and made it four in a row for Kaby, but I’m not a bit brave when things like my life are at stake.

Lili was starting to get up, acting a little dazed. Kaby gently pushed her down again and quietly said, “Where is it?” and then hauled off and slapped her across the face. What got me was the matter-of-fact way Kaby did it. I can understand somebody getting mad and socking someone, or even deliberately working up a rage so as to be able to do something nasty, but this cold-blooded way turns my stomach.

===Chinese===
克里特人的后脑勺发际底下也长着眼，要不就直说了吧：招待员到底不是士兵。卡比一侧身，顺手一拨，可怜的老莫德就去了她本要送卡比去的地方。眼看重力攥住莫德、把她猛拽下去，我一阵反胃。

我本可以跳起来，给卡比凑成四连击，可一牵扯到我这条命，我的勇敢就一星半点都不剩了。

莉莉正要起身，一副有点发懵的样子。卡比温温柔柔又把她按下去，安安静静地问：「它在哪儿？」随即抡起手，一记耳光抽上她的脸。叫我受不了的是卡比办事的那份就事论事。有人气昏了头揍人，甚至存心把自己的火气拱上来好下狠手，这我都懂，可这种冷血路数，我反胃。

===Original===
Lili looked as if half her face were about to start bleeding, but she didn’t look dazed any more and her jaw set. Kaby grabbed Lili’s pearl necklace and twisted it around her neck and it broke and the pearls went bouncing around like ping-pong balls, so Kaby yanked down Lili’s gray silk bandeau until it was around the neck and tightened that. Lili started to choke through her tight-pressed lips. Erich, Mark and Illy had come up and crowded around, but they seemed to be content with the job Kaby was doing.

“Listen, slut,” she said, “we have no time. You have a healing room in this place. I can work the things.”

“Here it comes,” I thought, wishing I could faint. On top of everything, on top of death even, they had to drag in the nightmare personally stylized for me, the horror with my name on it. I wasn’t going to be allowed to blow up peacefully. They weren’t satisfied with an A-bomb. They had to write my private hell into the script.

===Chinese===
莉莉那半边脸看着就要渗血了，可她不再发懵，下巴绷紧了。卡比一把揪住莉莉的珍珠项链，在她颈上绞了一圈，项链断了，珠子像乒乓球一样蹦得满地都是，于是卡比又把莉莉那条灰丝缎发带拽下来，勒上脖颈，收紧。莉莉从紧闭的双唇间开始抽气。埃里希、马克和伊尔利已经围拢过来，但对卡比手头的活计，他们似乎相当满意。

「听着，贱货，」她说，「我们没时间。这地方有一间治人的屋子。那些家什我会使。」

「来了。」我想着，巴望自己能晕过去。在这一切之上，在死亡之上，他们还非要拖出那场为我量身定做的噩梦，那件写着我名字的恐怖。我连被炸死都不配得个安生。一颗原子弹还不满足，他们还要把我的私人地狱写进剧本。

===Original===
“There is a thing called an Invertor,” Kaby said exactly as I’d known she would, but as I didn’t really hear it just then—a mental split I’ll explain in a moment. “It opens you up so they can cure your insides without cutting your skin or making you bleed anywhere. It turns the big parts of you inside out, but not the blood tubes. All your skin—your eyes, ears, nose, toes, all of it—becoming the lining of a little hole that’s half-filled with your hair.

“Meantime, your insides are exposed for whatever the healer wants to do to them. You live for a while on the air inside the hole. First the healer gives you an air that makes you sleep, or you go mad in about fifty heartbeats. We’ll see what ten heartbeats do to you without the sleepy air. Now will you talk?”

===Chinese===
「有一种东西，叫逆置器（Invertor），」卡比说，跟我料得分毫不差——不过那一刻我并没有真听见，这层精神上的分岔，我过一刻再解释。「它把你打开，好让人治你的内里，不切你的皮，不让你任何地方出血。它把你身上大的部件翻到外面，血管除外。你全部的皮肤——你的眼睛、耳朵、鼻子、脚趾，全部——变成一个小洞的内衬，洞里半填着你的头发。

「与此同时，你的内里敞开着，任凭治你的人处置。你靠洞里的空气活上一阵。治人的先给你一种让你睡着的空气，不然你约莫五十次心跳之内就会疯。咱们来看看，不给你睡气，十次心跳能把你怎样。现在你肯说了吗？」

---

===Original===
I hadn’t been listening to her, though, not the real me, or I’d have gone mad without getting the treatment. I once heard Doc say your liver is more mysterious and farther away from you than the stars, because although you live with your liver all your life, you never see it or learn to point to it instinctively, and the thought of someone messing around with that intimate yet unknown part of you is just too awful.

I knew I had to do something quick. Hell, at the first hint of Inversion, before Kaby had even named it, Illy had winced so that his tentacles were all drawn up like fat feather-sausages. Erich had looked at him questioningly, but that lousy Looney had un-endeared himself to me by squeaking, “Don’t mind me, I’m just sensitive. Get on with the girl. Make her tell.”

===Chinese===
不过我并没有在听她说话——真正的我没有，否则不用等上刑，我早疯了。我曾听大夫说过：你的肝比星辰更神秘、离你更远，因为尽管你与它终生相伴，你从没见过它，也学不会本能地指出它在哪儿；一想到有人去拨弄你身上这个亲密却未知的部件，就实在可怕。

我知道我得赶紧干点什么。见鬼，逆置的风声刚起，卡比还没说出名目，伊尔利就已经缩了一下，触手一根根收拢，像一排肥羽毛香肠。埃里希疑问地看了他一眼，可那个讨厌的月球佬吱吱出一句，把他在我这儿的最后一点人缘也吱没了：「别管我，我就是敏感。接着办那姑娘。叫她说。」

===Original===
Yes, I knew I had to do something, and here on the floor that meant thinking hard and in high gear about something else. The screwball sculpture Erich had tried to smash was a foot from my nose and I saw a faint trail of white stuff where it had skidded. I reached out and touched the trail; it was finely gritty, like powdered glass. I tipped up the sculpture and the part on which it had skidded wasn’t marred at all, not even dulled; the gray spheres were as glisteningly bright as ever. So I knew the trail was diamond dust rubbed off the diamonds in the floor by something even harder.

That told me the sculpture was something special and maybe Doc had had a real idea in his pickled brain when he’d been pushing the thing at all of us and trying to tell us something. He hadn’t managed to say anything then, but he had earlier when he’d been going to tell us what to do about the bomb, and maybe there was a connection.

I twisted my memory hard and let it spring back and I got “Inversh … bosh …” Bosh, indeed! Bosh and inverse bosh to all boozers, Russki or otherwise.

So I quick tried the memory trick again and this time I got “glovsh” and then I grasped and almost sneezed on diamond dust as I watched the pieces fit themselves together in my mind like a speeded-up movie reel.

===Chinese===
对，我知道得干点什么，而趴在地板上，这就意味着开足马力去拼命想点别的。埃里希刚才想砸没砸成的那座怪雕塑，离我的鼻子一英尺，它滑过的道上留着一抹淡淡的白痕。我伸手摸了摸那道痕；细细的颗粒感，像研碎的玻璃。我把雕塑翘起来，它滑过的那一面丝毫未损，连乌都不乌；灰球体依旧锃亮。于是我知道，那道白痕是钻石粉——被一样更硬的东西从地板的钻石上磨下来的。

这说明那座雕塑来头不小，没准大夫那颗腌透了的脑瓜里真揣着一个主意，才把那玩意儿朝我们每个人递、还想告诉我们什么。他当时没能说出一个字，可更早的时候他说出来过——就在他要告诉我们怎么处置炸弹那回，没准两桩事是连着的。

我把记忆使劲一拧，再松手弹回去，得了个「逆置……个屁……」。个屁，说得好！对天下一切酒鬼——俄国籍也罢，别国籍也罢——统统个屁，正着个反着也个屁。

于是我赶紧又玩了一遍那套记忆把戏，这回得到的是「手套……」，跟着我豁然贯通，看着一块块碎片在我脑子里像快进的胶片一样自行拼合，差点对着钻石粉打喷嚏。

===Original===
It all hung on that black right-hand hussar’s glove Lili had produced for Bruce. Only she couldn’t have found it in Stores, because we’d searched every fractional pigeonhole later on and there hadn’t been any gloves there, not even the left-hand mate there would have been. Also, Bruce had had two left-hand gloves to start with, and we had been through the whole Place with a fine-tooth comb, and there had been only the two black gloves on the floor where Bruce had kicked them off the bar—those two and those two only, the left-hand glove he’d brought from outside and the right-hand glove Lili had produced for him.

So a left-hand glove had disappeared—the last I’d seen of it, Lili had been putting it on her tray—and a right-hand glove had appeared. Which could only add up to one thing: Lili had turned the left-hand glove into an identical right. She couldn’t have done it by turning it inside out the ordinary way, because the lining was different.

===Chinese===
一切都系在莉莉变给布鲁斯的那只黑右手骠骑兵手套上。只是她不可能是从库房区找到的，因为后来我们连一格一格的鸽笼格都搜遍了，那儿根本没有任何手套，连本该存在的那只左手配对也没有。再者，布鲁斯起头手里是两只左手套，我们又拿篦子把整个场所篦过一遍，地板上只有布鲁斯从吧台踢下去的那两只黑手套——就那两只，仅那两只：他从外头带来的一只左手套，和莉莉变给他的一只右手套。

于是一只左手套消失了——我末一眼见它，是莉莉把它放上托盘——而一只右手套出现了。这只能归结为一件事：莉莉把那只左手套变成了一只一模一样的右手套。用寻常里外翻面的法子她办不到，因为衬里对不上。

---

===Original===
But as I knew only too sickeningly well, there was an extraordinary way to turn things inside out, things like human beings. You merely had to put them on the Invertor in Surgery and flick the switch for full Inversion.

Or you could flick it for partial Inversion and turn something into a perfect three-dimensional mirror image of itself, just what a right-hand glove is of a left. Rotation through the fourth dimension, the science boys call it; I’ve heard of it being used in surgery on the highly asymmetric Martians, and even to give a socially impeccable right hand to a man who’d lost one, by turning an amputated right arm into an amputated left.

Ordinarily, nothing but live things are ever Inverted in Surgery and you wouldn’t think of doing it to an inanimate object, especially in a Place where the Doc’s a drunk and the Surgery hasn’t been used for hundreds of sleeps.

But when you’ve just fallen in love, you think of wonderful crazy things to do for people. Drunk with love, Lili had taken Bruce’s extra left-hand glove into Surgery, partially Inverted it, and got a right-hand glove to give him.

What Doc had been trying to say with his “Inversh … bosh …” was “Invert the box,” meaning we should put the bronze chest through full Inversion to get at the bomb inside to disarm it. Doc too had got the idea from Lili’s trick with the glove. What an inside-out tactical atomic bomb would look like, I could not imagine and did not particularly care to see. I might have to, though, I realized.

===Chinese===
可正像我恶心到骨子里都知道的那样，有一种非常办法能把东西翻个里外——比如人这种东西。你只消把他放上手术区的逆置器，把开关一拨，全逆置。

或者拨到半逆置，把一件东西变成它自己的完美三维镜像——右手套之于左手套，正是如此。科学小子们管这叫「经第四维旋转」；我听说它用在过高度不对称的火星人身上做手术，甚至有人丢了右手，把截下来的右臂变成截下来的左臂，好给他安一只体面无懈可击的右手。

照规矩，手术区里从来只有活物才被逆置，谁也不会想到对一件死物下手，尤其在一个大夫是酒鬼、手术区几百年入眠没开过张的场所。

可人刚堕入爱河的时候，就会想出种种美妙的疯点子来讨人欢心。莉莉醉在爱里，把布鲁斯多出来的那只左手套带进手术区，半逆置了一下，得到一只右手套送他。

大夫那句「逆置……个屁……」想说的，其实是「把箱子逆置」，意思是：把青铜箱做一次全逆置，够到里面的炸弹拆掉它。大夫也是从莉莉的手套戏法里得的灵感。一只里外翻面的战术原子弹会是什么模样，我想象不出，也不怎么想见识。可我意识到，没准躲不掉。

===Original===
But the fast-motion film was still running in my head. Later on, Lili had decided like I had that her lover was going to lose out in his plea for mutiny unless she could give him a really captive audience—and maybe, even then, she had been figuring on creating the nest for Bruce’s chicks and … all those other things we’d believed in for a while. So she’d taken the Major Maintainer and remembered the glove, and not many seconds later, she had set down on a shelf of the Art Gallery an object that no one would think of questioning—except someone who knew the Gallery by heart.

I looked at the abstract sculpture a foot from my nose, at the clustered gray spheres the size of golf balls. I had known that the inside of the Maintainer was made up of vastly tough, vastly hard giant molecules, but I hadn’t realized they were quite *that* big.

I said to myself, “Greta, this is going to give you a major psychosis, but you’re the one who has to do it, because no one is going to listen to your deductions when they’re all practically living on negative time already.”

===Chinese===
可那卷快进胶片还在我脑子里放。再往后，莉莉断定——跟我猜的一样——她的爱人那场叛变陈情注定要输，除非她能给他一批货真价实、跑不掉的听众——而且没准，还在那时，她已经在盘算给布鲁斯的小雏鸟筑巢，还有……那些我们信过一阵的林林总总。于是她取走了主维持器，想起了手套，没过几秒，她就在艺术品廊的一格架子上放下了一件谁也不会起疑的东西——除非有谁把画廊背得滚瓜烂熟。

我看着离我鼻子一英尺的那座抽象雕塑，看着那团聚成堆的、高尔夫球大小的灰球体。我早知道维持器的内部由坚韧无比、坚硬无比的巨型分子构成，可我没意识到它们竟有*那*么大。

我对自己说：「格蕾塔，这一下够你得一场重型精神病了，可这事非你不可，因为他们个个实际上已经活在负时间里，没人会来听你的推理。」

---

===Original===
I got up as quietly as if I were getting out of a bed I shouldn’t have been in—there are some things Entertainers are good at—and Kaby was just saying “you go mad in about fifty heartbeats.” Everybody on their feet was looking at Lili. Sid seemed to have moved, but I had no time for him except to hope he hadn’t done anything that might attract attention to me.

I stepped out of my shoes and walked rapidly to Surgery—there’s one good thing about this hardest floor anywhere, it doesn’t creak. I walked through the Surgery screen that is like a wall of opaque, odorless cigarette smoke and I concentrated on remembering my snafued nurse’s training, and before I had time to panic, I had the sculpture positioned on the gleaming table of the Invertor.

I froze for a moment when I reached for the Inversion switch, thinking of the other time and trying to remember what it had been that bothered me so much about an inside-out brain being bigger and not having eyes, but then I either thumbed my nose at my nightmare or kissed my sanity goodbye, I don’t know which, and twisted the switch all the way over, and there was the Major Maintainer winking blue about three times a second as nice as you could want it.

It must have been working as sweet and steady as ever, all the time it was Inverted, except that, being inside out, it had hocused the direction finders.

===Chinese===
我起了身，安静得像从一张不该上的床里下床——有些事招待员就是擅长——卡比正好说到「你约莫五十次心跳之内就会疯」。站着的每个人都在看莉莉。西德像是挪动过，可我顾不上他，只盼他没干什么会引人看我的事。

我踢掉鞋，快步走向手术区——这全宇宙顶硬的地板有个好处：不吱呀。我穿过那面像一堵不透明、无味的烟墙似的手术区屏风，全神贯注召回我那笔乱成一锅粥的护士训练，还没来得及惊慌，已经把那座雕塑摆上了逆置器亮闪闪的台面。

伸手够逆置开关的那一瞬，我僵了僵，想起上一次，想回忆起「翻在外头、变大了、又没有眼睛的脑子」到底哪一点让我那么难受，随后我就要么是朝噩梦弹了个响鼻，要么是跟我的神智吻别了——分不清是哪个——把开关一拧到底，于是主维持器就在那儿，一秒钟大约闪三下蓝，要多称心有多称心。

它被逆置的这整段时间里，必定一直干得又稳又甜，只是因为里外翻了面，把测向仪们全蒙了。

## XV Lord Spider / 蜘蛛大人

===Original===
> black legged spiders
 

 with red hearts of hell

===Chinese===
> 黑腿的蜘蛛们，
> 心是地狱的红。

---

===Original===
“Jesu!” I turned and Sid’s face was sticking through the screen like a tinted bas-relief hanging on a gray wall and I got the impression he had peered unexpectedly through a slit in an arras into Queen Elizabeth’s bedroom.

He didn’t have any time to linger on the sensation, even if he’d wanted to, for an elbow with a copper band thrust through the screen and dug his ribs and Kaby marched Lili in by the neck. Erich, Mark and Illy were right behind. They caught the blue flashes and stopped dead, staring at the long-lost. Erich spared me one look which seemed to say, so you did it, not that it matters. Then he stepped forward and picked it up and held it solidly to his left side in the double right-angle made by fingers, forearm and chest, and reached for the Introversion switch with a look on his face as if he were opening a fifth of whisky.

===Chinese===
「耶稣啊！」我回头，西德的脸正从屏风里探过来，像一面挂在灰墙上着了色的浅浮雕，我得到的印象是：他无意间从挂毯的一道缝里，窥见了伊丽莎白女王的寝宫。

这滋味他没工夫细品，就算想品也没工夫，因为一只箍着铜环的胳膊肘穿过屏风，杵进他的肋骨，卡比掐着莉莉的脖子把她押了进来。埃里希、马克和伊尔利紧随其后。他们撞见那闪烁的蓝光，齐齐钉在原地，瞪着这件失踪已久的东西。埃里希分给我一瞥，那意思像是：原来是你干的，不过无所谓了。随后他上前拾起它，端端正正抵在左腰，手指、小臂和胸口摆出双重直角，伸手去够内闭拨杆，脸上的神情，像正要开一瓶威士忌。

===Original===
The blue light died and Change Winds hit me like a stiff drink that had been a long, long time in coming, like a hot trumpet note out of nowhere.

I felt the changing pasts blowing through me, and the uncertainties whistling past, and ice-stiff reality softening with all its duties and necessities, and the little memories shredding away and dancing off like autumn leaves, leaving maybe not even ghosts behind, and all the crazy moods like Mardi Gras dancers pouring down an evening street, and something inside me had the nerve to say it didn’t care whether Greta Forzane’s death was riding in those Winds because they felt so good.

I could tell it was hitting the others the same way. Even battered, tight-lipped Lili seemed to be saying, you’re making me drink the stuff and I hate you for it, but I do love it. I guess we’d all had the worry that even finding and Extroverting the Maintainer wouldn’t put us back in touch with the cosmos and give us those Winds we hate and love.

===Chinese===
蓝光熄灭，变更之风扑面砸来，像一杯等了太久太久的烈酒，像不知从哪儿拔起的一声滚烫的小号。

我感到变动不居的种种过去穿身而吹，种种不确定呼啸而过，冰僵硬挺的现实连同它全部的义务与必然软了下来，那些细小的记忆碎成一条条，像秋叶似的旋舞而去，兴许连幽灵都不留一个，还有种种疯癫的情绪，像狂欢节的舞者涌下一条夜晚的街道；我身体里竟有个东西大着胆子说：格蕾塔·福尔赞的死就算就骑在这些风里，它也不在乎——因为它们太舒服了。

我看得出来，这风砸在其他人身上也是一路货。就连挨了打、抿紧嘴的莉莉，看那样子也在说：你逼我喝这玩意儿，我恨你，可我是真爱喝。我猜我们大家原先都悬着一颗心：就算找回维持器、解了内闭，也未必接得上宇宙，未必还尝得到这阵我们又恨又爱的风。

===Original===
The thing that cut through to us as we stood there glowing was not the thought of the bomb, though that would have come in a few seconds more, but Sid’s voice. He was still standing in the screen, except that now his face was out the other side and we could just see parts of his gray-doubleted back, but, of course, his “Jesu!” came through the screen as if it weren’t there.

At first I couldn’t figure out who he could be talking to, but I swear I never heard his voice so courtly obsequious before, so strong and yet so filled with awe and an under-note of, yes, sheer terror.

“Lord, I am filled from top to toe with confusion that you should so honor my poor Place,” he said. “Poor say I and mine, when I mean that I have ever busked it faithfully for you, not dreaming that you would ever condescend … yet knowing that your eye was certes ever upon me … though I am but as a poor pinch of dust adrift between the suns … I abase myself. Prithee, how may I serve thee, sir? I know not e’en how most suitably to address thee, Lord … King … Emperor Spider!”

===Chinese===
我们一个个红光满面站在那儿，先刺穿进来的是西德的声音，不是对炸弹的念想——虽说再过几秒它也会到。他还站在屏风里，只是这回脸伸到了另一侧外头，我们只望得见他灰紧身衣背影的一部分，可他那声「耶稣啊！」当然是穿屏而过，仿佛屏风不存在。

起初我猜不出他可能在跟谁讲话，可我发誓，我从没听他用过这般宫廷式的谄媚嗓音，那么洪亮，却又盛满敬畏，底下还压着一层——没错——赤裸裸的恐惧。

「大人，您这般光临鄙所，我上上下下涨满了惶恐，」他说。「我说『鄙』，也说我自己：我一直为您尽心梳妆，做梦也不敢想您竟肯屈尊......却也明知您的眼睛确实始终看顾着我......虽然我不过是漂泊在群阳之间的一撮可怜尘土......我自贬自抑。求您示下：小人何以效劳，老爷？我甚至不知该怎样称呼您才最合体统，大人......大王......蜘蛛皇帝！」

---

===Original===
I felt like I was getting very small, but not a bit less visible, worse luck, and even with the Change Winds inside me to give me courage, I thought this was really too much, coming on top of everything else; it was simply unfair.

At the same time, I realized it was to be expected that the big bosses would have been watching us with their unblinking beady black eyes ever since we had Introverted waiting to pounce if we should ever come out of it. I tried to picture what was on the other side of the screen and I didn’t like the assignment.

But in spite of being petrified, I had a hard time not giggling, like the zany at graduation exercises, at the way the other ones in Surgery were taking it.

I mean the Soldiers. They each stiffened up like they had the old ramrod inside them, and their faces got that important look, and they glanced at each other and the floor without lowering their heads, as if they were measuring the distance between their feet and mentally chalking alternate sets of footprints to step into. The way Erich and Kaby held the Major and Minor Maintainers became formal; the way they checked their Callers and nodded reassuringly was positively esoteric. Even Illy somehow managed to look as if he were on parade.

===Chinese===
我觉得自己正缩得极小，可见度却一分不减，真倒霉，哪怕体内灌着变更之风给我壮胆，我也觉得这实在过了：什么都赶在这一头压下来；简直不讲道理。

与此同时，我也明白这在情理之中：打我们内闭那刻起，大头目们必定就用他们那不眨的黑豆眼盯死了我们，只等我们露头便扑。我试着想象屏风另一侧是什么，结果不喜欢这份差事。

可尽管吓得石化，我还是憋笑憋得辛苦，活像毕业典礼上的活宝——笑的是手术区里另外几位的那副受用相。

我说的是那几位士兵。他们一个个绷直了，像体内插回了老式通条，脸上挂起那种事关重大的神色，头也不低地互相扫视、扫视地板，仿佛在丈量自己双脚的间距，在心里用粉笔默画出一套套可以踩进去的脚印。埃里希和卡比握持主、副维持器的姿势变得仪态庄严；他们核对呼叫器、宽慰地点头的做派，简直秘仪十足。连伊尔利都不知怎么摆出了一副受阅的架势。

===Original===
Then from beyond the screen came what was, under the circumstances, the worst noise I’ve ever heard, a seemingly wordless distant-sounding howling and wailing, with a note of menace that made me shake, although it also had a nasty familiarity about it I couldn’t place. Sid’s voice broke into it, loud, fast and frightened.

“Your pardon, Lord, I did not think … certes, the gravity … I’ll attend to it on the instant.” He whipped a hand and half a head back through the screen, but without looking back and snapped his fingers, and before I could blink, Kaby had put the Minor Maintainer in his hand.

Sid went completely out of sight then and the howling stopped, and I thought that if that was the way a Lord Spider expressed his annoyance at being subjected to incorrect gravity, I hoped the bosses wouldn’t start any conversations with me.

Erich pursed his lips and threw the other Soldiers a nod and the four of them marched through the screen as if they’d drilled a lifetime for this moment. I had the wild idea that Erich might give me his arm, but he strode past me as if I were … an Entertainer.

I hesitated a moment then, but I had to see what was happening outside, even if I got eaten up for it. Besides, I had a bit of the thought that if these formalities went on much longer, even a Lord Spider was going to discover just how immune he was to confined atomic blast.

I walked through the screen with Lili beside me.

===Chinese===
随后，屏风那边传来了就当时情形而言我这辈子听过的最糟的声响：一阵听似无语词、像是从远处传来的嗥叫哀嚎，带着一股叫我发抖的威胁，虽说其中又有一种讨厌的耳熟，我一时对不上号。西德的声音插了进去，又响、又急、又怕。

「您恕罪，大人，我没有想到......确实，那重力......我这就去料理。」他把手和半个脑袋唰地抽回屏风这侧，头也不回地打了个响指，我还没眨完眼，副维持器已经进了他的手心。

西德随即整个人没了影，嗥叫也停了，我心想：倘若蜘蛛大人受了不合规格的重力、要这样表达不快，那但愿头目们别来找我搭话。

埃里希抿了抿唇，朝其他士兵一点头，四人齐步穿过屏风，仿佛为这一刻操练了一辈子。我闪过一个妄想：埃里希没准会让我挽他的胳膊，可他大步从我身边走过，仿佛我是......一个招待员。

我犹豫了一下，可外面在发生什么，我非看不可，哪怕被一口吞了。再说，我还捏着一点念头：这套仪礼再拖长些，连蜘蛛大人也要亲自发现，他对密闭空间里的原子爆炸免疫到什么程度了。

我穿过屏风，莉莉走在我旁边。

---

===Original===
The Soldiers had stopped a few feet in front of it. I looked around ahead for whatever it was going to turn out to be, prepared to drop a curtsy or whatever else, bar nothing, that seemed expected of me.

I had a hard time spotting the beast. Some of the others seemed to be having trouble too. I saw Doc weaving around foolishly by the control divan, and Bruce and Beau and Sevensee and Maud on their feet beyond it, and I wondered whether we were dealing with an invisible monster; ought to be easy enough for the bosses to turn a simple trick like invisibility.

Then I looked sharply left where everyone else, even glassy-eyed Doc, was coming to look, into the Door sector, only there wasn’t any monster there or even a Door, but just Siddy holding the Minor Maintainer and grinning like when he is threatening to tickle me, only more fiendishly.

“Not a move, masters,” he cried, his eyes dancing, “or I’ll pin the pack of you down, marry and amen I will. It is my firm purpose to see the Place blasted before I let this instrument out of my hands again.”

===Chinese===
士兵们在它前面几步停住了。我朝前方环视，找那东西究竟会是什么，预备好屈膝行礼或别的什么，无所不尽，只要看着像是期待我做的。

那头巨兽我找了半天。其余几位似乎也在找。我看见大夫在控制躺椅边傻乎乎地摇晃，布鲁斯、博、七观和莫德在躺椅那头站着，我琢磨：咱们对付的会不会是一头隐形的怪物；对头目们来说，隐身这种小戏法还不是信手拈来。

随后我利落地朝左看——其余所有人，连眼神发直的大夫，都正朝那边看——看进门区，可那儿根本没有怪物，连门都没有，只有西迪，手里托着副维持器，咧着嘴笑，像他要挠我痒痒时的那副笑，只是更邪乎。

「都别动，各位师傅，」他高喝，眼珠乱转，「不然我就把你们一锅钉在地上，皇天在上，阿门，我说到做到。宁可眼看场所炸上天，我也绝不再让这台家什离开我的手——此志不移。」

===Original===
My first thought was, “ ’Sblood but Siddy is a real actor! I don’t care if he didn’t study under anyone later than Burbage, that just proves how good Burbage is.”

Sid had convinced us not only that the real Spiders had arrived, but earlier that the gravity in the edge of Stores had been a lot heavier than it actually was. He completely fooled all those Soldiers, including my swelled-headed victorious little commandant, and I kind of filed away the timing of that business of reaching out the hand and snapping the fingers without looking, it was so good.

“Beauregard!” Sid called. “Get to the Major Maintainer and call headquarters. But don’t come through Door, marry go by Refresher. I’ll not trust a single Demon of you in this sector with me until much more has been shown and settled.”

“Siddy, you’re wonderful,” I said, starting toward him. “As soon as I got the Maintainer unsnarled and looked around and saw your sweet old face—”

“Back, tricksy trull! Not the breadth of one scarlet toenail nearer me, you Queen of Sleights and High Priestess of Deception!” he bellowed. “You least of all do I trust. Why you hid the Maintainer, I know not, ’faith, but later you’ll discover the truth to me or I’ll have your gizzard.”

I could see there was going to have to be a little explaining.

===Chinese===
我的头一个念头是：「皇天作证，西迪真是位好演员！他师从的师父再晚也晚不过伯比奇（Burbage），我不在乎——那恰恰证明伯比奇有多棒。」

西德不但让我们信了真蜘蛛驾到，先前还让我们信了库房区边缘的重力比实际重得多。他把那几位士兵骗了个通透，包括我那位翘尾巴的胜利者小指挥官，而那记背身伸手、响指不停顿的时机功夫，我默默收进了档案——实在漂亮。

「博雷加德！」西德喊。「到主维持器那儿去，呼叫总部。但别走门区，皇天在上，绕盥洗区走。在这个区里，一个魔鬼我信不过，跟你同处更不行——在更多东西亮出来、说清楚之前。」

「西迪，你太了不起了，」我说着朝他走去。「我刚把维持器解出来，一抬眼就看见你那张可亲的老脸——」

「退下，刁钻的小贱人！不许再近我一枚红指甲盖的宽度，你这诡计女王、欺瞒大祭司！」他咆哮。「我最信不过的就是你。你为何藏起维持器，说真的我不知道，可回头你得不把真相吐给我，不然我挖你的杂碎。」

我看得出来，回头免不了要解释几句。

---

===Original===
Doc, touched off, I guess, by Sid waving his hand at me, threw back his head and let off one of those shuddery Siberian wolf-howls he does so blamed well. Sid waved toward him sharply and he shut up, beaming toothily, but at least I knew who was responsible for the Spider wail of displeasure that Sid had either called for or more likely got as a gift of the gods and used in his act.

Beau came circling around fast and Erich shoved the Major Maintainer into his hands without making any fuss. The four Soldiers were looking pretty glum after losing their grand review.

Beau dumped some junk off one of the Art Gallery’s sturdy taborets and set the Major Maintainer on it carefully but fast, and quickly knelt in front of it and whipped on some earphones and started to tune. The way he did it snatched away from me my inward glory at my big Inversion brainwave so fast, I might never have had it, and there was nothing in my mind again but the bronze bomb chest.

I wondered if I should suggest Inverting the thing, but I said to myself, “Uh-uh, Greta, you got no diploma to show them and there probably isn’t time to try two things, anyway.”

Then Erich for once did something I wanted him to, though I didn’t care for its effect on my nerves, by looking at his Caller and saying quietly, “Nine minutes to go, if Place time and cosmic time are synching.”

Beau was steady as a rock and working adjustments so fine that I couldn’t even see his fingers move.

===Chinese===
大夫呢，我猜是被西德朝我挥手的动作点着了，仰起头，放出了那种他拿手得要命的、叫人起鸡皮疙瘩的西伯利亚狼嚎。西德朝他猛一挥手，他就收声，龇着一口牙直乐，可至少我知道了：那声表达不悦的「蜘蛛哀鸣」该谢谁——那是西德要么现点、更可能是天赐的礼物，被他顺手编进了戏里。

博飞快绕了过来，埃里希把主维持器塞进他手里，没摆一点架子。盛大阅兵泡了汤，四位士兵看上去都相当蔫。

博把艺术品廊一张结实小圆柜上的杂物一掸，又快又稳地把主维持器搁上去，随即跪在它跟前，唰地扣上耳机，开始调频。他那一手快得把我心里那点「逆置妙计」的自得整个卷走了，快得仿佛我压根没得意过，我脑子里又只剩那口青铜炸弹箱了。

我琢磨着要不要提议把那东西逆置一下，可我对自己说：「不行不行，格蕾塔，你手上没有文凭给他们看，再说多半也没工夫两样都试。」

随后埃里希破天荒做了件我盼他做的事——虽说它对我神经的杀伤我不领教——他看了眼呼叫器，轻声说：「还剩九分钟，前提是场所时间和宇宙时间对得上拍。」

博稳如磐石，调校的动作细得我连他手指的起落都看不见。

===Original===
Then, at the other end of the Place, Bruce took a few steps toward us. Sevensee and Maud followed a bit behind him. I remembered Bruce was another of our nuts with a private program for blowing up the place.

“Sidney,” he called, and then, when he’d got Sid’s attention, “Remember, Sidney, you and I both came down to London from Peterhouse.”

I didn’t get it. Then Bruce looked toward Erich with a devil-may-care challenge and toward Lili as if he were asking her forgiveness for something. I couldn’t read her expression; the bruises were blue on her throat and her cheek was puffy.

Then Bruce once more shot Erich that look of challenge and he spun and grabbed Sevensee by a wrist and stuck out a foot—even half-horses aren’t too sharp about infighting, I guess, and the satyr had every right to feel at least as confused as I felt—and sent him stumbling into Maud, and the two of them tumbled to the floor in a jumble of hairy legs and pearl-gray frock. Bruce raced to the bomb chest.

===Chinese===
随后，在场所另一头，布鲁斯朝我们走了几步。七观和莫德缀在他身后一点。我想起来，布鲁斯也是我们这伙疯子里的一个，兜里揣着一份把这里炸上天的私人计划。

「悉尼，」他喊，等西德注意到他，又说：「记着，悉尼，你和我，都是从彼得豪斯（Peterhouse）下来的伦敦。」

我没听懂。随后布鲁斯朝埃里希投去一个满不在乎的挑战，又望向莉莉，仿佛在为什么事求她原谅。她的表情我读不出来；她喉咙上的淤伤青着，脸颊肿着。

随后布鲁斯再一次朝埃里希抛去那记挑衅，随即一转身，攥住七观一只手腕，伸出一脚——半马人对近身缠斗大约就是不灵，再说这半人羊就算跟我一样晕头转向也全在情理——把他搡得跌进莫德怀里，两个滚倒在地板上，毛腿和珠灰小裙缠作一堆。布鲁斯直奔炸弹箱。

---

===Original===
Most of us yelled, “Stop him, Sid, pin him down,” or something like that—I know I did because I was suddenly sure that he’d been asking Lili’s pardon for blowing the two of them up—and all the rest of us too, the love-blinded stinker.

Sid had been watching him all the time and now he lifted his hand to the Minor Maintainer, but then he didn’t touch any of the dials, just watched and waited, and I thought, “Shaitan shave us! Does Siddy want in on death, too? Ain’t he satisfied with all he knows about life?”

Bruce had knelt and was twisting some things on the front of the chest, and it was all as bright as if he were under a bank of Klieg lights, and I was telling myself I wouldn’t know anything when the fireball fired, and not believing it, and Sevensee and Maud had got unscrambled and were starting for Bruce, and the rest of us were yelling at Sid, except that Erich was just looking at Bruce very happily, and Sid was still not doing anything, and it was unbearable except just then I felt the little arteries start to burst in my brain like a string of firecrackers and the old aorta pop, and for good measure, a couple of valves come unhinged in my ticker, and I was thinking, “Well, now I know what it’s like to die of heart failure and high blood pressure,” and having a last quiet smile at having cheated the bomb, when Bruce jumped up and back from the chest.

“That does it!” he announced cheerily. “She’s as safe as the Bank of England.”

Sevensee and Maud stopped themselves just short of knocking him down and I said to myself, “Hey, let’s get a move on! I thought heart attacks were fast.”

===Chinese===
我们大多数人都喊：「拦住他，西德，把他钉下来！」或类似的话——我知道我喊了，因为我突然笃定，他刚才是在求莉莉原谅——原谅他把他们俩一起炸上天——外带我们所有人，这个被爱情晃瞎眼的坏蛋。

西德一直盯着他，这时抬手向副维持器，可又没碰任何旋钮，只看着，等着，我心想：「筛坦（Shaitan，阿拉伯语的魔鬼）剃了咱们吧！西迪也想掺一份死亡不成？他知道的生还不够多吗？」

布鲁斯已经跪下，在箱子正面拧动一些什么，而这一切亮堂堂的，仿佛他顶着一排弧光灯；我一面告诉自己火球点火时我什么都来不及知道，一面又信不过这句话；七观和莫德已经解开了缠绊，正朝布鲁斯扑去；我们其余的人都在朝西德喊，只有埃里希正喜滋滋地看着布鲁斯，西德仍旧按兵不动；这一切都熬不住了，偏在这时，我感到脑子里的小动脉开始一串鞭炮似的炸开，老主动脉砰地爆裂，外搭心脏里两片瓣膜脱了榫，我正想着：「好啊，这回我可知道死于心力衰竭加高血压是什么滋味了，」正为骗过了炸弹露出最后一个安详的微笑——布鲁斯从箱子前一跃而起，向后跳开。

「搞定！」他喜气洋洋地宣布。「它现在跟英格兰银行一样安全。」

七观和莫德堪堪刹住，没把他扑倒，我对自己说：「嘿，倒是快点啊！我还当心脏病发作是很快的呢。」

===Original===
Before anyone else could speak, Beau did. He had turned around from the Major Maintainer and pulled aside one of the earphones.

“I got headquarters,” he said crisply. “They told me how to disarm the bomb—I merely said I thought we ought to know. What did you do, sir?” he called to Bruce.

“There’s a row of four ankhs just below the lock. The first to your left you give a quarter turn to the right, the second a quarter turn to the left, same for the fourth, and you don’t touch the third.”

“That is it, sir,” Beau confirmed.

The long silence was too much for me; I guess I must have the shortest span for unspoken relief going. I drew some nourishment out of my restored arteries into my brain cells and yelled, “Siddy, I know I’m a tricksy trull and the High Vixen of all Foxes, but what the Hell is Peterhouse?”

“The oldest college at Cambridge,” he told me rather coolly.

===Chinese===
不等别人开口，博开了口。他从主维持器前转过身，摘开一只耳机。

「我接通了总部，」他干脆利落地说。「他们告诉了我拆弹的法子——我只说了一句：我想咱们应该知道。您是怎么做的，先生？」他朝布鲁斯喊。

「锁的正下方有一排四个安卡。从你左边数第一个向右拧四分之一圈，第二个向左四分之一圈，第四个同样，第三个碰都别碰。」

「分毫不差，先生。」博证实。

这漫长的沉默我熬不住了；论「无言的如释重负」的耐受时长，我八成是全场最短的。我从复原的动脉里抽了点养分输进脑细胞，嚷道：「西迪，我知道我是刁钻的小贱人、群狐中的首席雌狐狸，可彼得豪斯到底是个什么鬼？」

「剑桥最古老的学院。」他相当冷淡地告诉我。

## XVI The Possibility-Binders / 可能性束缚者

===Original===
> Familiar with infinite universe sheafs and open-ended postulate systems?—the notion that everything is possible—and I mean everything—and everything has happened. *Everything.*

===Chinese===
> 对「无限宇宙束」和「开放式公理系统」熟不熟？——那种认为一切皆可能的观念——我说的可是一切——而且一切都已发生过。*一切*。

---

===Original===
An hour later, I was nursing a weak highball and a black eye in the sleepy-time darkness on the couch farthest from the piano, half watching the highlighted party going on around it and the bar, while the Place waited for rendezvous with Egypt and the Battle of Alexandria.

Sid had swept all our outstanding problems into one big bundle and, since his hand held the joker of the Minor Maintainer, he had settled them all as high-handedly as if they’d been those of a bunch of schoolkids.

It amounted to this:

We’d been Introverted when most of the damning things had happened, so presumably only we knew about them, and we were all in so deep one way or another that we’d all have to keep quiet to protect our delicate complexions.

Well, Erich’s triggering the bomb did balance rather neatly Bruce’s incitement to mutiny, and there was Doc’s drinking, while everybody who had declared for the peace message had something to hide. Mark and Kaby I felt inclined to trust anywhere, Maud for sure, and Erich in this particular matter, damn him. Illy I didn’t feel at all easy about, but I told myself there always has to be a fly in the ointment—a darn big one this time, and furry.

Sid didn’t mention his own dirty linen, but he knew we knew he’d flopped badly as boss of the Place and only recouped himself by that last-minute flimflam.

Remembering Sid’s trick made me think for a moment about the real Spiders. Just before I snuck out of Surgery, I’d had a vivid picture of what they must look like, but now I couldn’t get it again. It depressed me, not being able to remember—oh, I probably just imagined I’d had a picture, like a hophead on a secret-of-the-universe kick. Me ever find out anything about the Spiders?—except for nervous notions like I’d had during the recent fracas?—what a laugh!

The funniest thing (ha-ha!) was that I had ended up the least-trusted person. Sid wouldn’t give me time to explain how I’d deduced what had happened to the Maintainer, and even when Lili spoke up and admitted hiding it, she acted so bored I don’t think everybody believed her—although she did spill the realistic detail that she hadn’t used partial Inversion on the glove; she’d just turned it inside out to make it a right and then done a full Inversion to get the lining back inside.

===Chinese===
一个钟头后，我窝在离钢琴顶远的那张躺椅上，在昏昏欲睡的暗处，养着一杯淡威士忌和一只乌青的眼眶，半看半不看那场围着钢琴和吧台打得火亮的派对，而场所正等着与埃及、与亚历山大港战役会合。

西德把我们所有悬而未决的难题扫作一大捆，又因为王牌——副维持器——攥在他手里，便一股脑儿专断裁断，仿佛处理的不过是一群小学生的纠纷。

归结起来是这样：

那些要命的勾当大半发生时我们处于内闭状态，所以大概只有我们自己知情，而我们人人都在这潭或那潭浑水里陷得够深，只好集体缄口，护住各自娇嫩的体面。

呃，埃里希引爆炸弹这一桩，跟布鲁斯煽动叛变那一桩，正好半斤八两；外加大夫的酗酒，而凡是为和平讯息表过态的，个个都有短处要藏。马克和卡比，我天南地北都信得过；莫德更不必说；埃里希——就这一件事上——也信得过，该死的。伊尔利我可怎么都不踏实，可我劝自己：膏药里总得有只苍蝇——这回是只特大号的，还带毛。

西德没提他自己那件脏衣裳，可他知道我们知道：他这个场所老板当得一败涂地，全靠临了那手空空妙计才扳回一城。

想起西德那手戏法，我有一阵子又琢磨起真蜘蛛来。溜出手术区之前，我对他们必然的长相有过一幅活生生的图景，可现在再也要不回来了。想不起来，这叫我丧气——哦，八成我根本只是想象自己有过一幅图景，就像瘾君子嗑上了「宇宙终极奥秘」那一口。我还想查出蜘蛛的底细？——除了方才混战里那种神经质的念头？——笑活人！

顶好笑的事（哈哈！）是：到头来我成了最没人信的那个。西德不肯给我时间解释我怎么推出了维持器的下落，而等莉莉开口承认是她藏的，她那副爱答不理的做派，我看未必人人都信——虽说她确实漏了个写实的细节：手套那回她没使半逆置；她只是把它里外翻成一只右手，然后做了一次全逆置，把衬里翻了回去。

---

===Original===
I tried to get Doc to confirm that he’d reasoned the thing out the same way I had, but he said he had been blacked out the whole time, except during the first part of the hunt, and he didn’t remember having any bright ideas at all. Right now, he was having Maud explain to him twice, in detail, everything that had happened. I decided that it was going to take a little more work before my reputation as a great detective was established.

I looked over the edge of the couch and just made out in the gloom one of Bruce’s black gloves. It must have been kicked there. I fished it up. It was the right-hand one. My big clue, and was I sick of it! Got mittens, God forbid! I slung it away and, like a lurking octopus, Illy shot up a tentacle from the next couch, where I hadn’t known he was resting, and snatched the glove like it was a morsel of underwater garbage. These E.T.s can seem pretty shuddery nonhuman at times.

I thought of what a cold-blooded, skin-saving louse Illy had been, and about Sid and his easy suspicions, and Erich and my black eye, and how, as usual, I’d got left alone in the end. My men!

Bruce had explained about being an A-tech. Like a lot of us, he’d had several widely different jobs during his first weeks in the Change World and one of them had been as secretary to a group of the minor atomics boys from the Manhattan-Project-Earth-Satellite days. I gathered he’d also absorbed some of his bothersome ideas from them. I hadn’t quite decided yet what species of heroic heel he belonged to, but he was thick with Mark and Erich again. Everybody’s men!

Sid didn’t have to argue with anybody; all the wild compulsions and mighty resolves were dead now, anyway until they’d had a good long rest. I sure could use one myself, I knew.

===Chinese===
我想让大夫确认他是跟我同一条思路推出真相的，可他说自己一路断片，只有搜查前半段醒着，而且压根不记得有过什么灵光。眼下，他正让莫德把发生的一切详详细细给他讲上两遍。我断定：要确立我大侦探的声望，还得多下点功夫。

我从躺椅边沿望下去，昏暗里勉强认出布鲁斯的一只黑手套。准是被人踢到那儿去的。我捞起来。是那只右手的。我的头号线索，而我见了它就腻！连指手套是拿到了，上帝可饶了我吧！我把它甩出去，隔壁躺椅上立刻像埋伏的章鱼似的射出一条触手——我先前根本不知道伊尔利歇在那儿——把那只手套当一粒水下垃圾似的叼走了。这些 E.T. 有时候显得真叫人发毛地非人。

我想起伊尔利那条保皮救命的冷血虱子做派，想起西德和他那副张口就来的疑心，想起埃里希和我的乌眼青，想起自己到头来，一如既往，又落了单。我的男人们！

原子技师的事，布鲁斯解释过了。跟我们中许多人一样，进变更世界的头几个星期里他换过好几种毫不相干的差事，其中一样，是给一群曼哈顿计划-地球卫星年代的小字辈原子小子当秘书。我估摸，他那几样惹麻烦的主意也是从他们那儿吸来的。他到底属于哪一属哪一种的英雄型浑球，我还没拿定主意，反正他又跟马克和埃里希打得火热了。大家的男人们！

西德跟谁都用不着争；那些狂热的冲动和豪壮的决心如今都死透了，至少也得好好长休一场才能缓过来。我自己就很能来上一场，这我知道。

===Original===
The party at the piano was getting wilder. Lili had been dancing the black bottom on top of it and now she jumped down into Sid’s and Sevensee’s arms, taking a long time about it. She’d been drinking a lot and her little gray dress looked about as innocent on her as diapers would on Nell Gwyn. She continued her dance, distributing her marks of favor equally between Sid, Erich and the satyr. Beau didn’t mind a bit, but serenely pounded out “Tonight’s the Night”—which she’d practically shouted to him not two minutes ago.

I was glad to be out of the party. Who can compete with a highly experienced, utterly disillusioned seventeen-year-old really throwing herself away for the first time?

===Chinese===
钢琴边的派对越烧越野。莉莉方才一直在琴顶上跳黑臀舞（black bottom），这会儿跳下来，落进西德和七观的臂弯，落了老大一会儿。她酒喝了不少，那条小灰裙穿在她身上那份清纯，约等于尿布穿在内尔·格温（Nell Gwyn，查理二世的名伶情妇）身上。她接着跳，把恩宠的印记均分给西德、埃里希和半人羊。博一点不介意，安之若素地敲出《今夜正是时候》（Tonight's the Night）——不到两分钟前她差不多是喊着点给他的。

我庆幸自己退出了派对。谁争得过一位经验老到、幻灭到底的十七岁姑娘——头一回真正地自抛自掷？

---

===Original===
Something touched my hand. Illy had stretched a tentacle into a furry wire to return me the black glove, although he ought to have known I didn’t want it. I pushed it away, privately calling Illy a washed-out moronic tarantula, and right away I felt a little guilty. What right had I to be critical of Illy? Would my own character have shown to advantage if I’d been locked in with eleven octopoids a billion years away? For that matter, where did I get off being critical of anyone?

Still, I was glad to be out of the party, though I kept on watching it. Bruce was drinking alone at the bar. Once Sid had gone over to him and they’d had one together and I’d heard Bruce reciting from Rupert Brooke those deliberately corny lines, “For England’s the one land, I know, Where men with Splendid Hearts may go; and Cambridgeshire, of all England, The Shire for Men who Understand;” and I’d remembered that Brooke too had died young in World War One and my ideas had got fuzzy. But mostly Bruce was just calmly drinking by himself. Every once in a while Lili would look at him and stop dead in her dancing and laugh.

I’d figured out this Bruce-Lili-Erich business as well as I cared to. Lili had wanted the nest with all her heart and nothing else would ever satisfy her, and now she’d go to hell her own way and probably die of Bright’s disease for a third time in the Change World. Bruce hadn’t wanted the nest or Lili as much as he wanted the Change World and the chances it gave for Soldierly cavorting and poetic drunks; Lili’s seed wasn’t his idea of healing the cosmos; maybe he’d make a real mutiny some day, but more likely he’d stick to barroom epics.

His and Lili’s infatuation wouldn’t die completely, no matter how rancid it looked right now. The real-love angle might go, but Change would magnify the romance angle and it might seem to them like a big thing of a sort if they met again.

===Chinese===
有什么碰了碰我的手。伊尔利把一条触手抻成一根毛绒绒的细线，把黑手套递还给我，尽管他该知道我不要它。我推开它，心里暗骂伊尔利是一只褪了色的白痴狼蛛，随即又有点心虚。我有什么资格数落伊尔利？把我跟十一个八爪怪锁在一起，隔上十亿年，我那点品性就能拿得出手？再说了，我有什么资格数落任何人？

话虽如此，我庆幸自己退了场，眼睛却一直没离开。布鲁斯独自在吧台喝酒。有一回西德踱过去，两人共饮了一杯，我听见布鲁斯朗诵鲁珀特·布鲁克那些存心发腻的句子：「因为英格兰是我知道的唯一地方，壮心之士尽可前往；而全英格兰数剑桥郡，最配懂行的人们安身」（译注：布鲁克《格兰切斯特老牧师宅》）——我又想起布鲁克同样年纪轻轻死在第一次世界大战，我的念头就发起浑来。但多数时候，布鲁斯只是安安静静地独酌。偶尔莉莉会望他一眼，舞步当场一刹，笑出声来。

布鲁斯-莉莉-埃里希这摊子事，我琢磨到不想再琢磨的地步了。莉莉是一心一意要那个窝，别的什么也喂不饱她，往后她会沿着自己的路下地狱，多半还会在变更世界里第三次死于布赖特氏病。布鲁斯呢，要那个窝、要莉莉，都赶不上他要变更世界，赶不上它给的士兵式撒欢和诗人式烂醉；莉莉的种子，不合他疗愈宇宙的口味；没准哪天他真会干一场像样的兵变，但更大的可能是守着酒馆史诗过日子。

他和莉莉这段痴恋不会死绝，不管眼下看着多馊。真爱那一面兴许会磨掉，可变更会把罗曼史那一面放大，倘若他们重逢，没准会觉得那是另一种意义上的大事件。

===Original===
Erich had his *Kamerad*, shaped to suit him, who’d had the guts and cleverness to disarm the bomb he’d had the guts to trigger. You have to hand it to Erich for having the nerve to put us all in a situation where we’d have to find the Maintainer or fry, but I don’t know anything disgusting enough to hand to him.

I had tried a while back. I had gone up behind him and said, “Hey, how’s my wicked little commandant? Forgotten your *und so weiter*?” and as he turned, I clawed my nails and slammed him across the cheek. That’s how I got the black eye. Maud wanted to put an electronic leech on it, but I took the old handkerchief in ice water. Well, at any rate Erich had his scratches to match Bruce’s, not as deep, but four of them, and I told myself maybe they’d get infected—I hadn’t washed my hands since the hunt. Not that Erich doesn’t love scars.

===Chinese===
埃里希有了他的*Kamerad*（战友），量身合体：有胆量拆掉他有胆量引爆的炸弹，还有那份聪明。不得不服埃里希敢把我们全体逼进「找不出维持器就下油锅」的绝地——至于还要再奉送他点什么，我想不出世上有什么够恶心的。

前一阵我试过了。我从他背后凑上去，说：「嘿，我的邪恶小指挥官怎么样了？忘了你的*und so weiter*（等等等等）啦？」趁他转身，我一爪子挠下去，甩了他一脸颊。我的乌眼青就是这么来的。莫德要给我上电子水蛭，我只要了老式的冰水手帕。总之，埃里希脸上也有了抓痕，跟布鲁斯那道配成对：没那么深，但足足四道，我安慰自己，没准会感染——搜查之后我就没洗过手。倒不是说埃里希不爱疤。

---

===Original===
Mark was the one who helped me up after Erich knocked me down.

“You got any omnias for that?” I snapped at him.

“For what?” Mark asked.

“Oh, for everything that’s been happening to us,” I told him disgustedly.

He seemed to actually think for a moment and then he said, “*Omnia mutantur, nihil interit.*”

“Meaning?” I asked him.

He said, “All things change, but nothing is really lost.”

It would be a wonderful philosophy to stand with against the Change Winds. Also damn silly. I wondered if Mark really believed it. I wished I could. Sometimes I come close to thinking it’s a lot of baloney trying to be any decent kind of Demon, even a good Entertainer. Then I tell myself, “That’s life, Greta. You’ve got to love through it somehow.” But there are times when some of these cookies are not too easy to love.

Something brushed the palm of my hand again. It was Illy’s tentacle, with the tendrils of the tip spread out like a little bush. I started to pull my hand away, but then I realized the Loon was simply lonely. I surrendered my hand to the patterned gossamer pressures of feather-talk.

Right away I got the words, “Feeling lonely, Greta girl?”

It almost floored me, I tell you. Here I was understanding feather-talk, which I just didn’t, and I was understanding it in English, which didn’t make sense at all.

For a second, I thought Illy must have spoken, but I knew he hadn’t, and for a couple more seconds I thought he was working telepathy on me, using the feather-talk as cues. Then I tumbled to what was happening: he was playing English on my palm like on the keyboard of his squeakbox, and since I could play English on a squeakbox myself, my mind translated automatically.

Realizing this almost gave my mind stage fright, but I was too fagged to be hocused by self-consciousness. I just lay back and let the thoughts come through. It’s good to have someone talk to you, even an underweight octopus, and without the squeaks Illy didn’t sound so silly; his phrasing was soberer.

===Chinese===
埃里希把我撂倒后，扶我起来的是马克。

「你有什么 omnia 能治这个吗？」我冲他抢白。

「治什么？」马克问。

「哦，治砸到咱们头上的这一切。」我厌恶地告诉他。

他像是认认真真想了一想，然后说：「*Omnia mutantur, nihil interit.*」

「什么意思？」我问他。

他说：「万物皆变，无一真正消亡。」

拿这套哲学迎着变更之风站着，倒也壮观。也蠢得可以。我怀疑马克是不是真信。我倒希望自己能信。有时候我险些觉得，想当个体面样的魔鬼——哪怕只当个好招待员——纯属瞎扯。这时我就告诉自己：「这就是生活，格蕾塔。你总得想法子爱着穿过它。」可有些时候，这伙老家伙里的一些，实在爱不起来。

又有什么拂过我的掌心。是伊尔利的触手，末梢的绒须张开成一丛小灌木。我正要缩手，随即明白过来：这月球佬不过是寂寞。我把手交了出去，交给羽语那一阵一阵蛛纱似的轻压。

我立刻「听」到了字句：「觉得孤单吗，格蕾塔姑娘？」

我跟你说，这一下差点把我撂倒。我居然懂了羽语——我根本不懂的——而且是用英语懂的——这压根讲不通。

有一秒，我以为伊尔利开了口，可我知道他没有；又有两秒，我以为他在对我传心，拿羽语当提示。随后我恍然明白了怎么回事：他在我的掌心上敲英语，就像敲他那台吱吱匣的键盘，而既然我自己也能在吱吱匣上敲英语，我的脑子就自动翻译了。

明白这一层，几乎叫我的脑子怯场，可我累过头了，没力气被这份自觉唬住。我只管往后一靠，任那些念头流进来。有人跟你说话总是好的，哪怕是一只体重不达标的章鱼，而且没了那股吱吱声，伊尔利听着不那么傻气了；他的措辞沉稳了许多。

---

===Original===
“Feeling sad, Greta girl, because you’ll never understand what’s happening to us all,” Illy asked me, “because you’ll never be anything but a shadow fighting shadows—and trying to love shadows in between the battles? It’s time you understood we’re not really fighting a war at all, although it looks that way, but going through a kind of evolution, though not exactly the kind Erich had in mind.

“Your Terran thought has a word for it and a theory for it—a theory that recurs on many worlds. It’s about the four orders of life: Plants, Animals, Men and Demons. Plants are energy-binders—they can’t move through space or time, but they can clutch energy and transform it. Animals are space-binders—they can move through space. Man (Terran or E.T., Lunan or non-Lunan) is a time-binder—he has memory.

“Demons are the fourth order of evolution, possibility-binders—they can make all of what might be part of what is, and that is their evolutionary function. Resurrection is like the metamorphosis of a caterpillar into a butterfly: a third-order being breaks out of the chrysalis of its lifeline into fourth-order life. The leap from the ripped cocoon of an unchanging reality is like the first animal’s leap when he ceases to be a plant, and the Change World is the core of meaning behind the many myths of immortality.

“All evolution looks like a war at first—octopoids against monopoids, mammals against reptiles. And it has a necessary dialectic: there must be the thesis—we call it Snake—and the antithesis—Spider—before there can be the ultimate synthesis, when all possibilities are fully realized in one ultimate universe. The Change War isn’t the blind destruction it seems.

“Remember that the Serpent is your symbol of wisdom and the Spider your sign for patience. The two names are rightly frightening to you, for all high existence is a mixture of horror and delight. And don’t be surprised, Greta girl, at the range of my words and thoughts; in a way, I’ve had a billion years to study Terra and learn her languages and myths.

“Who are the real Spiders and Snakes, meaning who were the first possibility-binders? Who was Adam, Greta girl? Who was Cain? Who were Eve and Lilith?

“In binding all possibility, the Demons also bind the mental with the material. All fourth-order beings live inside and outside all minds, throughout the whole cosmos. Even this Place is, after its fashion, a giant brain: its floor is the brainpan, the boundary of the Void is the cortex of gray matter—yes, even the Major and Minor Maintainers are analogues of the pineal and pituitary glands, which in some form sustain all nervous systems.

“There’s the real picture, Greta girl.”

The feather-talk faded out and Illy’s tendril tips merged into a soft pad on which I fingered, “Thanks, Daddy Longlegs.”

===Chinese===
「觉得难过吗，格蕾塔姑娘，因为你永远弄不明白我们大家身上在发生什么，」伊尔利问我，「因为你永远只能是一个影子同影子搏斗——还要在战斗的间隙里去爱影子？你早该明白：我们根本不是在打仗——尽管看着像——我们是在经历一种演化，虽说不是埃里希心里想的那种。

「你们的地球思想里有一个词、一套理论——一套在许多世界上反复出现的理论。它讲生命的四个阶序：植物、动物、人与魔鬼。植物是能量束缚者——它们不能在空间或时间中移动，却能攫取能量并转化它。动物是空间束缚者——它们能在空间中移动。人（地球人也罢，E.T. 也罢，月球人也罢，非月球人也罢）是时间束缚者——他有记忆。

「魔鬼是第四阶的演化，可能性束缚者——他们能把一切『可能』变成『实是』的一部分，这正是他们的演化职能。复活好比毛虫化为蝴蝶的蜕变：一个第三阶的存在，破开它生命线的茧壳，进入第四阶的生命。从不变现实那被撕裂的茧衣中一跃而出，就像第一只动物不再做植物时的那一跃，而变更世界，是无数不朽神话背后那颗意义的核。

「一切演化起初都像一场战争——八足类打单足类，哺乳类打爬行类。它有一种必要的辩证法：必得先有正题——我们称之为蛇——再有反题——蜘蛛——然后才有终极的合题，那时一切可能性都将在一个终极宇宙中圆满实现。变更战争并不是它看起来的那场盲目毁灭。

「记住，蛇是你们象征智慧的符号，蜘蛛是你们象征耐心的记号。这两个名字令你们理所应当地恐惧，因为一切高级的存在，都是恐怖与欢愉的混合。也别惊讶我言辞与思想的幅员，格蕾塔姑娘；从某种意义上说，我用了十亿年来研究地球，学习她的语言和神话。

「谁是真正的蜘蛛和蛇——也就是说，谁是第一批可能性束缚者？谁是亚当，格蕾塔姑娘？谁是该隐？夏娃和莉莉丝又是谁？

「在束缚一切可能性的同时，魔鬼们也把心灵与物质束缚在一起。一切第四阶的存在，都活在全部心智的里里外外，遍布整个宇宙。就连这处场所，以它自己的方式，也是一颗巨脑：地板是颅腔，虚空的边界是灰质的皮层——是的，连主维持器和副维持器，也是松果腺与脑垂体的对应物，它们以某种形态维系着一切神经系统。

「这才是真正的图景，格蕾塔姑娘。」

羽语淡了下去，伊尔利的绒须尖并成一片软垫，我在上面敲：「谢了，长腿蛛爹爹。」

---

===Original===
Chewing over in my mind what Illy had just told me, I looked back at the gang around the piano. The party seemed to be breaking up; at least some of them were chopping away at it. Sid had gone to the control divan and was getting set to tune in Egypt. Mark and Kaby were there with him, all bursting with eagerness and the vision of tanks on ranks of mounted Zombie bowmen going up in a mushroom cloud; I thought of what Illy had told me and I managed a smile—seems we’ve got to win and lose all the battles, every which way.

Mark had just put on his Parthian costume, groaning cheerfully, “Trousers again!” and was striding around under a hat like a fur-lined ice-cream cone and with the sleeves of his metal-stuffed candys flapping over his hands. He waved a short sword with a heart-shaped guard at Bruce and Erich and told them to get a move on.

Kaby was going along on the operation wearing the old-woman disguise intended for Benson-Carter. I got a halfhearted kick out of knowing she was going to have to cover that chest and hobble.

Bruce and Erich weren’t taking orders from Mark just yet. Erich went over and said something to Bruce at the bar, and Bruce got down and went over with Erich to the piano, and Erich tapped Beau on the shoulder and leaned over and said something to him, and Beau nodded and yanked “Limehouse Blues” to a fast close and started another piece, something slow and nostalgic.

Erich and Bruce waved to Mark and smiled, as if to show him that whether he came over and stood with them or not, the legate and the lieutenant and the commandant were very much together. And while Sevensee hugged Lili with a simple enthusiasm that made me wonder why I’ve wasted so much imagination on genetic treatments for him, Erich and Bruce sang:

===Chinese===
心里嚼着伊尔利刚告诉我的一切，我回望钢琴边那伙人。派对像是散了；至少有几位正在动手拆它。西德已到控制躺椅那儿，正准备调去埃及。马克和卡比陪在他身边，一个个涨满了热望，涨满了坦克碾过一列列骑马僵尸弓手、蘑菇云腾起的美景；我想起伊尔利的话，勉强笑了笑——看来这些仗咱们是输也得输、赢也得赢，四面八方地输赢。

马克刚套上他那身帕提亚行头，快活地哼哼：「又穿裤子！」顶着一顶毛皮衬里的冰淇淋筒似的帽子大步流星，塞了金属的坎迪斯宽袍（candys，帕提亚贵族的塞棉宽袖袍）袖子盖过手背扑扑飞。他朝布鲁斯和埃里希挥着一把心形护手的短剑，催他们快着点。

卡比也将随队出征，穿的是原本备给本森-卡特的老妇装。一想到她得罩住那身胸甲、装驼背蹒跚，我闷闷地乐了半声。

布鲁斯和埃里希这会儿还没到听马克号令的地步。埃里希走到吧台边对布鲁斯说了句什么，布鲁斯下来，随他走到钢琴边，埃里希拍了拍博的肩，俯身对他说了句什么，博点点头，把《莱姆豪斯布鲁斯》（Limehouse Blues）利落地收了个快板尾，另起了一支曲子，慢的，怀旧的。

埃里希和布鲁斯朝马克挥手微笑，仿佛要让他看明白：他过不过来站在一起，使者、中尉和指挥官都铁板一块。趁着七观以一股朴素的热情搂抱莉莉——看得我纳闷，自己先前何必为他的基因疗程浪费那么多想象力——埃里希和布鲁斯唱了起来：

===Original===
> “To the legion of the lost ones, to the cohort of the damned,
 

 To our brothers in the tunnels outside time,
 

 Sing three Change-resistant Zombies, raised from death and robot-crammed,
 

 And Commandos of the Spiders—
 

 Here’s to crime!
 

 We’re three blind mice on the wrong time-track,
 

 Hush—hush—hush!
 

 We’ve lost our now and will never get back,
 

 Hush—hush—hush!
 

 Change Commandos out on the spree,
 

 Damned through all possibility,
 

 Ghostgirls, think kindly on such as we,
 

 Hush—hush—hush!”

===Chinese===
> 「致迷途者的军团，致被诅咒者的支队，
> 致时间之外坑道里的众兄弟，
> 唱一支三个抗变更的僵尸，死而复起、肚里塞满机器，
> 又是蜘蛛的突击队——
> 敬罪恶一杯！
> 我们是错误时间轨上的三只瞎老鼠，
> 嘘——嘘——嘘！
> 丢掉了现在，永远回不了头，
> 嘘——嘘——嘘！
> 变更突击队出来撒欢，
> 受诅咒贯穿一切可能性，
> 幽灵少女们，垂怜我们这般人，
> 嘘——嘘——嘘！」（译注：仿罗伯特·W. 瑟维斯《迷途者军团》。）

===Original===
While they were singing, I looked down at my charcoal skirt and over at Maud and Lili and I thought, “Three gray hustlers for three black hussars, that’s our speed.” Well, I’d never thought of myself as a high-speed job, winning all the races—I wouldn’t feel comfortable that way. Come to think of it, we’ve got to lose and win all the races in the long run, the way the course is laid out.

I fingered to Illy, “That’s the picture, all right, Spider boy.”

===Chinese===
他们唱着，我低头看看自己的炭灰裙子，又望望莫德和莉莉，心想：「三个灰扑扑的勤快姑娘，配三名黑衣骠骑兵——这就是咱们的配速。」呃，我从不把自己当成什么高速货色，场场必赢——那样我反而不自在。细想想，照这条跑道的铺法，长线看去，这些赛咱们也终归是输也输得、赢也赢得。

我用指头对伊尔利敲道：「画面就是这样了，没错，蜘蛛小子。」
